Function add_effect(h, pos, neg, p, delta): with h in state old, add delta * derivative contributions. delta can +1 or -1. For candidate apply: add_effect(i,+1), add_effect(j,-1). For reject after restoring h: add_effect(i,-1), add_effect(j,+1). For accept no rollback. This works regardless of h state if h is the state prior to that delta, and other variables maybe? If apply two effects sequentially, first effect uses h old; second effect should also use h old for its slices, but if we update h after first? The total finite change of swapping both variables: c_new - c_old = effect_i(old) + effect_j(old), where effect_j uses h values before swap, including h_i old. If we change h after first, second using new h at i would differ if |i-j|=d; so we should compute/apply both effects using old h before changing h. def apply_pair(h, pos, neg, i, j, t):
   # h old, t mass i += t, j -= t
   p=i; delta=t; slices...
   p=j; delta=-t; slices...

   # then update h
   h[i] += t; h[j] -= t

Phase 5: Final polish continuous near best with very small steps and high-effort local search.

Let's implement active gradient/heuristic:
- E = max(c0,pos,neg). Define threshold `E - small_tol` (e.g. 0.5 count). Indices d where c close. Also c0. Build list of active types/weights maybe. For each active lag, add gradient contributions to score array `score` (length n): For each d with type pos, weight w. For pos effect per unit increase at p: plus - h[p-d] for d≤p, plus (1-h[p+d]) for p+d<n. Actually gradient partials for pos: as p first: (1-h[p+d]); as p second: -h[p-d]. For neg: as first: (1-h[p-d]); as second: -h[p+d]. c0: 1-2h[p]. Multiply by weights. We can compute score by looping active lags and adding slices:
   if pos[d]: w; score[1:d? wait d]
For pos[d] (d>0):
   # p+d valid: p=0..n-d-1 => first term (1-h[p+d]) add to score[p]
   # p-d valid: p=d..n-1 => second -h[p-d] add to score[p]
So:
   score[:n-d] += w * (1 - h[d:])
   score[d:] += -w * h[:n-d]
For neg[d]:
   # p-d valid: p=d..n-1 first (1-h[p-d])
   score[d:] += w * (1 - h[:n-d])
   # p+d valid: p=0..n-d-1 second -h[p+d]
   score[:n-d] += -w * h[d:]
c0: score += w0*(1-2h).
This is O(active_count * n). Active count maybe up to 100. For n=400, 40k ops per recomputation. We can recompute after improvements or every X iterations. Then choose pairs: sort score, each iteration pick i random among bottom quantile (low score) and j among top quantile (high). Move from j to i: if h_i<h_j? So low/negative score: increase variable regardless of current value if <1. High/positive: decrease if >0. Thus if h_i<h_j? not necessary. For score_i negative but h_i=0.8, can decrease? If score_i negative and score_j positive, but h_i>h_j? t positive max limited by 1-h_i maybe small; could still. If h_i close 1, move small. If not enough, choose differently. Simpler: choose i low score and j high score, set t sign = + if (h_i < 1 and h_j >0) and maybe prefer if derivative of score? score = compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,E)  # zero-sum? not needed
low_idx = np.argsort(score)[:n//4]
high_idx = np.argsort(score)[-n//4:]
for iter:
   choose i from low_idx, j from high_idx (random)
   if i==j continue
   if score_i < score_j: tmax = min(1-h[i], h[j]) else tmax = min(h[i], 1-h[j]) negative sign
   if tmax small skip
   choose t fraction (e.g. 0.25,0.5,1.0 of tmax, maybe random)
   evaluate; if E<best apply, recompute score occasionally; else count_no
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kw):

 try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=len(h)
 except: n=400; h=make_random_feasible(n,seed)
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 pos,neg,c0=compute_counts(h,n)
 E=max(...); bound=E*dx
 best_h=h.copy(); best_E=E; best_n=n
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 # ensure binary base? If h not feasible? project.
 # Phase A: guided continuous local search (deterministic-ish) for ~500s
 improve_guided(h,n,counts,deadline_A,mode='continuous',seed)
 # Update best
 # Phase B: binary local search: convert best_h to binary n=400, run guided binary/anneal for ~200s
 h_b=to_binary(best_h,n)
 counts_b=compute_counts...
 run guided(binary t=1, maybe score) deadline_B

 # Phase C: other n: For n2 in [300,500,250] if time; interpolate best_h (continuous) to n2, project, compute counts, run guided shorter; if lower update best.
 # Also random restarts at n=400: add small noise to best_h, project, run guided shorter.
 # Final polish: guided with very small t for remaining time on best_n.
 return best_h, best_E*2/best_n, best_n

Need define helper `guided_search(h,n,deadline,seed, binary=False, verbose?)` returns h, E, counts. It can modify in place and keep counts.

Implement `guided_search`:
Inputs h length n, c0,pos,neg maybe. We can recompute counts inside to ensure.
Pseudo:
```
def guided_search(h, n, deadline, seed, binary=False, max_iters=10**9):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n)
    E = current_E(c0,pos,neg)
    dx = 2.0/n
    T = 0.3  # count units for acceptance of worse
    cool = 0.9999
    no_improve = 0
    score = compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,E,n, tol=0.75)
    low_count = max(20,n//8)
    high_count = max(20,n//8)
    # maybe use rank arrays updated when score recompute
    rank = np.argsort(score)
    low_idx = rank[:low_count]
    high_idx = rank[-high_count:]
    while time<deadline:
       if no_improve > 5000: recompute score; adjust T? maybe T = max(0.05, T*0.8); no_improve=0
       i=int(rng.choice(low_idx)); j=int(rng.choice(high_idx))
       if i==j: continue
       # Determine sign/direction to reduce score: if score[i] < score[j], t positive else negative.
       if score[i] <= score[j]:
           tmax = min(1-h[i], h[j])
           sign=1
       else:
           tmax = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
           sign=-1
       if tmax <= 1e-12: 
           # fallback random pair
           p,q = rng.choice(n), rng.choice(n); if p!=q ...
       if binary:
           # only if h_i and h_j can transfer fully? For binary, choose i zero, j one for positive, or vice versa.
           if sign>0:
               if h[i] <= 0.5 and h[j] >=0.5: t=1
               else skip
           else:
               if h[i] >=0.5 and h[j] <=0.5: t=-1
               else skip
       else:
           # choose fraction
           if rng.random()<0.15: frac = 1.0
           elif rng.random()<0.5: frac=0.5
           else: frac=0.1*rng.random()+0.05? 
           # Maybe also random explore: with prob 0.2 pick t uniform [0.01,1]*tmax
           t=sign*tmax*frac
           if abs(t)<1e-8 skip
       # Save and apply
       old_h_i=h[i]; old_h_j=h[j]; old_c0=c0; pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy()
       new_c0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,i,j,t)
       new_E = max(new_c0, pos.max(), neg.max())
       if new_E < E - 1e-12:
           E=new_E; c0=new_c0; no_improve=0; if rng.random()<0.02 maybe recompute score? (if improvement) score=compute_score...
       else:
           if T > 1e-6 and rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0,E-new_E)/T):
               E=new_E; c0=new_c0; no_improve += 0? accept worse but maybe no_improve? no change best; count as step, T cool; if new_E<E (not) no. Accept worse can help escape; maybe no_improve += 1.
           else:
               # rollback
               pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; h[i]=old_h_i; h[j]=old_h_j; c0=old_c0

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def project_feasible(h,n):
    # bisect tau
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    # But h values in [0,1], adding tau range [-1,1] enough? To reach sum target, tau between -max(h) and 1-min(h), within [-1,1]
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # fix tiny sum by adjusting largest room
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
       else: order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       for idx in order:
          if diff>0:
             amt=min(1-h[idx], diff); h[idx]+=amt; diff-=amt
          else:
             amt=min(h[idx], -diff); h[idx]-=amt; diff+=amt
          if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return h
```
Bisection 60*400 operations =24k, fine. It projects exactly via sorting fix.

`compute_counts`:
```
def compute_counts(h,n):
    c0=float(np.dot(h,1.0-h))
    pos=np.zeros(n,float); neg=np.zeros(n,float)
    # use for d in range(1,n):
    # maybe vectorize with np.correlate? For safety direct.
    for d in range(1,n):
       pos[d]=float(np.dot(h[:n-d], 1.0-h[d:]))
       neg[d]=float(np.dot(h[d:], 1.0-h[:n-d]))
    return c0,pos,neg
```
This loops; for n=400 okay. For many calls (resample) fine. Could use `one_h = 1-h`.

`apply_pair` as above. Need use old h for slice values. Since we pass h and it will be updated at end. It uses h old; we must not modify h before. Good.
```
def apply_pair(h,pos,neg,i,j,t):
    # t nonzero
    n=len(h)
    c0_old = ? Need c0 passed. We'll pass c0.
    hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
    p=i; dlt=t
    if p>0:
       rev=h[:p][::-1]
       pos[1:p+1] -= dlt*rev
       neg[1:p+1] += dlt*(1.0-rev)
    if p<n-1:
       plus=h[p+1:]
       pos[1:n-p] += dlt*(1.0-plus)
       neg[1:n-p] -= dlt*plus
    p=j; dlt=-t
    if p>0:
       rev=h[:p][::-1]
       pos[1:p+1] -= dlt*rev
       neg[1:p+1] += dlt*(1.0-rev)
    if p<n-1:
       plus=h[p+1:]
       pos[1:n-p] += dlt*(1.0-plus)
       neg[1:n-p] -= dlt*plus
    d=abs(i-j)
    if d>0:
       tt=t*t
       pos[d]+=tt; neg[d]+=tt
    new_c0 = c0 + t*(1.0-2.0*hi) - t*(1.0-2.0*hj) - 2.0*t*t
    h[i] = hi + t
    h[j] = hj - t
    return new_c0
```
Check signs for p=j dlt=-t:
- pos first part: -= dlt*rev = -(-t)rev = +t rev? But for p=j Δ=-t, pos as second derivative = -Δ h[p-d] = +t h; yes.
- pos plus: += dlt(1-plus)= -t(1-plus); first derivative Δ(1-h)= -t(...); good.
- neg first: += dlt(1-rev)= -t(1-rev); Δ(1-h)=-t; good.
- neg plus: -= dlt plus = +t plus; -Δ h = +t h; good.
Cross always +t². Good.
Need if i and j adjacent distance d where pos/neg update slices may already include interaction? We add cross always. Good.

def to_binary(h,n):
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    b=np.zeros(n,float); b[idx[:n//2]]=1.0
    return b
```

`compute_score` top-level:
```
def compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,E,n,tol=0.75):
    score=np.zeros(n,float)
    thresh=E-tol
    w0 = max(0.0, c0 - thresh)
    if w0>0: score += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    any_active = w0>0
    for d in range(1,n):
       w1=pos[d]-thresh
       if w1>0:
          w1 -=? weight maybe w1 or 1. Use w1 (closeness). Could use 1 for equal; if E near max. Let's use w1 if >0.
          score[:n-d] += w1*(1.0-h[d:])
          score[d:] -= w1*h[:n-d]
          any_active=True
       w2=neg[d]-thresh
       if w2>0:
          score[d:] += w2*(1.0-h[:n-d])
          score[:n-d] -= w2*h[d:]
          any_active=True
    if not any_active:
       # fallback: include c0 and top 3 pos/neg unweighted
       score += (1.0-2.0*h)
       # find top 3 unique lags? simply top d by pos/neg values
       idxp=np.argsort(pos)[::-1][:3]
       idxn=np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]
       for d in idxp:
          if d>0:
             score[:n-d] += 1.0-h[d:]; score[d:] -= h[:n-d]
       for d in idxn:
          if d>0:
             score[d:] += 1.0-h[:n-d]; score[:n-d] -= h[d:]
    # center score? Subtract mean? Pair move sign uses relative; center not needed. Maybe subtract mean to zero for symmetric.
    score -= score.mean()
    return score
```
Using weights `w1` can be tiny for near active; okay. If tol=0.75, weight ≤0.75; scale irrelevant.

`guided_continuous` function:
Need careful to return best_h, best_E, best_counts? We can recompute counts for best at end. We'll pass h, n, deadline, seed, binary flag maybe, initial_t? We'll track best.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def guided_search(h, n, deadline, seed, binary=False, allow_worse=False, tol=0.75, iters_limit=10000000):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n)
    curE = current_energy(...)
    best_E = curE
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c0,best_pos,best_neg = c0,pos.copy(),neg.copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    score = compute_score(...)
    rank = np.argsort(score)
    k = max(10, n//6)
    low_idx = rank[:k]
    high_idx = rank[-k:]
    T = 0.25 if allow_worse else 0.0
    cool=0.99985
    steps=0
    no_improve=0
    # if binary, optional lists
    while time.time()<deadline and steps<iters_limit:
       # update score periodically
       if steps % 2000 == 0:
          score=compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,tol)
          rank=np.argsort(score)
          low_idx=rank[:k]; high_idx=rank[-k:]
       i=int(rng.choice(low_idx)); j=int(rng.choice(high_idx))
       if i==j: continue
       if binary:
          hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
          if hi <= 0.5 and hj >= 0.5:
              t=1.0
          elif hi >=0.5 and hj <=0.5:
              t=-1.0
          else: continue
          # For binary, direction maybe if t positive but score_j < score_i? We can still try; maybe choose sign by feasibility only.
       else:
          # consider best of two directions? Choose sign based on score, but also if that direction infeasible use opposite.
          if score[i] <= score[j]:
              tmax = min(1.0-h[i], h[j]); tsign=1.0
              if tmax<=1e-12:
                 tmax = min(h[i],1.0-h[j]); tsign=-1.0
          else:
              tmax = min(h[i],1.0-h[j]); tsign=-1.0
              if tmax<=1e-12:
                 tmax = min(1.0-h[i],h[j]); tsign=1.0
          if tmax<=1e-12: continue
          # Choose fraction. For deterministic descent, maybe try several fractions? We can one candidate.
          # Use a schedule: first 50% time use frac choices [0.25,0.5,1.0]; later smaller.
          r=rng.random()
          if r<0.2: frac=1.0
          elif r<0.5: frac=0.5
          elif r<0.8: frac=0.2
          else: frac=0.05
          # If allowing worse, occasional larger random
          if allow_worse and rng.random()<0.1: frac=rng.random()
          t=tsign*tmax*frac
          if abs(t)<1e-9: continue
       oldh_i=h[i]; oldh_j=h[j]; oldc0=c0
       pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy()
       newc0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,i,j,t)
       newE = max(newc0, pos.max(), neg.max())
       oldE=curE
       if newE < oldE - 1e-12:
          curE=newE; c0=newc0; no_improve=0
          if newE < best_E:
             best_E=newE; best_h=h.copy(); best_c0=newc0; best_pos=pos.copy(); best_neg=neg.copy()
          if rng.random()<0.05 or no_improve==0 and steps%50==0? recompute score immediately maybe
          score=compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,tol)
          rank=np.argsort(score); low_idx=rank[:k]; high_idx=rank[-k:]
       else:
          accepted=False
          if allow_worse and T>1e-6:
             dE = newE - oldE
             if dE <=0: accepted=True
             elif rng.random() < math.exp(-min(dE, 20.0)/T): accepted=True
          if accepted:
             # allows worse: update current but not best? If newE<best update best. If newE<oldE already handled.
             curE=newE; c0=newc0
             if newE < best_E:
                best_E=newE; best_h=h.copy(); best_c0=newc0; best_pos=pos.copy(); best_neg=neg.copy()
             no_improve += 1
          else:
             pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; h[i]=oldh_i; h[j]=oldh_j; c0=oldc0
             # no_improve? maybe count rejections
             no_improve += 1
       T = max(1e-4, T*cool)
       # If no_improve too large, maybe reheat or reset
       if no_improve > 8000:
          # recompute score
          score=compute_score(...); rank...
          no_improve=0
          if allow_worse: T=max(T,0.1)
          # If current much worse than best and budget remaining, maybe revert to best
          if curE > best_E + 3.0 and time.time() < deadline-10:
             h[:] = best_h; c0,best? recompute counts
             c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n); curE=best_E
       steps +=1
       # FAIL?
    # End: return best_h, best_E, best counts? To let caller continue from best.
    return best_h, best_E
```

Could implement a simple active-lag reducing greedy:
- Get list of lags at max E (or within 0.1). For each active lag type d, identify pairs (p,q) that contribute to the overlap: for pos[d], contributing pairs are h_p=1, h_q=0 with q=p+d. A swap that directly reduces pos[d] by 1: either turn p from 1 to0 (swap p with a zero) or turn q from0 to1 (swap q with a one), while not increasing other active lags. We can find such. But score method approximates.

Let's test the update formula with continuous and cross more thoroughly.
For arbitrary h, c_pos[d]=Σ_a h_a(1-h_{a+d}). Let change Δ vector. New c = old + linear (Σ Δ_p (1-h_{p+d}) for p+d<n) + (Σ -Δ_p h_{p-d} for p-d≥0) + cross terms for pairs where both changed in same monomial. For same monomial x_p(1-h_{p+d}), if both p and p+d changed: term change old - new = Δ_p - Δ_p h_{p+d,old} - Δ_{p+d} h_{p,old} - Δ_p Δ_{p+d}. Linear terms include Δ_p(1-h_{p+d}) and -Δ_{p+d} h_p. Cross = -Δ_p Δ_{p+d}. For pair swap Δ_i=t, Δ_j=-t. If j=i+d, Δ_p=t, Δ_q=-t -> cross=-(-t²)=+t². If i>j (p=j, q=i), Δ_p=-t, Δ_q=t -> cross= -(-t²)=+t². So always +t² for each monomial. Same for each d where distance. Only one pos monomial for distance d=|i-j| and one neg monomial. Good. c0: monomials x_p(1-x_p), if p=i or j: cross -Δ² each, so -2t². Good.

Thus apply_pair exact.

Now, when we rollback by restoring h and pos/neg, we don't need inverse cross. Good.

`pos` and `neg` arrays are counts (not multiplied dx). c0 too.
Initial `compute_counts` using dot: for d, pos[d] = dot(h[:n-d], 1-h[d:]) = Σ_{a=0}^{n-d-1} h_a (1-h_{a+d}). neg[d] = dot(h[d:], 1-h[:n-d]) = Σ_{a=d}^{n-1} h_a (1-h_{a-d}). Good.
`compute_score` formulas match gradients for pos/neg:
 pos[d]: partial wrt h_p:
    if p+d<n: (1-h_{p+d}) => p=0..n-d-1: score[p] += w*(1-h[p+d]); slice score[:n-d]+=w*(1-h[d:])
    if p-d>=0: -h_{p-d} => p=d..n-1: score[p]-=w*h[p-d]; slice score[d:]-=w*h[:n-d]
 neg[d]: partial:
    if p-d>=0: (1-h_{p-d}) => p=d..n-1: score[d:]+=w*(1-h[:n-d])
    if p+d<n: -h_{p+d} => p=0..n-d-1: score[:n-d]-=w*h[d:]
Correct.

```
def search_binary(h, n, deadline, seed):
    h=to_binary(h,n)
    c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n)
    curE=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_E=curE
    score=compute_score(...)
    zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0]; ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
    k=max(10,n//6)
    z_low=zeros[np.argsort(score[zeros])[:k]]
    o_high=ones[np.argsort(score[ones])[::-1][:k]]
    T=0.7; cool=0.9999; noimp=0; steps=0
    while time<deadline:
       if steps%1000==0:
          recompute score; update zeros/ones and candidate arrays
       i=rng.choice(z_low); j=rng.choice(o_high); if i==j continue
       old... pos_old=...
       newc0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,i,j,1.0)
       newE=...
       if newE<curE:
          curE=newE; noimp=0; update best; if steps%20==0 recompute score/cands
       else:
          if T>1e-3 and rng.random()<exp(-(newE-curE)/T):
             curE=newE; noimp+=1; if newE<best update; else rollback; noimp+=1
          else rollback
       T=max(0.02,T*cool)
       if noimp>10000:
          # reheat/reset
          T=0.5; noimp=0; recompute
          if curE > best_E+3: reset h=best_h+random flips? project
       steps+=1
    return best_h,best_E
```
Because binary `curE` integer. Accepting worse with T cool. If no improvement for 10000, reheat. If current too worse, reset to best and flip random 5-20 pairs to new basin. Flipping pairs: since h binary and sum fixed, choose random zero/one and apply_pair t=1 (accept regardless), maybe 10 flips. Then recompute score. This allows escape.
Do we need to track `best_pos/neg`? We just store best_h and best_E counts. At reset, set h=best_h.copy(); c0,pos,neg=compute_counts; curE=best_E; then random flips maybe.
At end, return best_h; counts recompute outside.

def search_continuous(h,n,deadline,seed,anneal=True):
    ... score-based low/high all indices

      hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
      # primary direction: if score_i <= score_j: positive (increase i decrease j) else negative

         if tpos>1e-11: t=tps*frac else if tneg>...: t=-tneg*frac else skip
      else:
         tneg=...

def search_continuous(h,n,deadline,seed,anneal=False,tries=1):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n)
    curE=max(...)
    best_E=curE; best_h=h.copy(); best_c0=curE? no need best counts
    score=compute_score(...)
    rank=np.argsort(score)
    k=max(20,n//5)
    low=rank[:k]; high=rank[-k:]
    T=0.15 if anneal else 0.0
    cool=0.9998
    noimp=0; steps=0
    eps=1e-12
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if steps % 1000 ==0:
          score=compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n)
          rank=np.argsort(score); low=rank[:k]; high=rank[-k:]
       i=int(rng.choice(low)); j=int(rng.choice(high))
       if i==j:
           steps+=1; continue
       hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
       # determine primary sign and max_abs
       if score[i] <= score[j]:
           tpos = min(max(0.0,1.0-hi), max(0.0,hj))
           tneg = min(max(0.0,hi), max(0.0,1.0-hj))
           if tpos > eps:
              sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
           elif tneg > eps:
              sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
           else:
              steps+=1; continue
       else:
           tneg = min(max(0.0,hi), max(0.0,1.0-hj))
           tpos = min(max(0.0,1.0-hi), max(0.0,hj))
           if tneg > eps:
              sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
           elif tpos > eps:
              sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
           else:
              steps+=1; continue
       # choose fractions
       if tries == 1:
          fr = 1.0 if rng.random()<0.2 else (0.5 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0.2)
          if anneal and rng.random()<0.15: fr = rng.random()
          t = sign*tmax*fr
          # evaluate one
          oldh_i=hi; oldh_j=hj; oldc0=c0; pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy()

          newE=max(newc0,pos.max(),neg.max())
          accepted = (newE < curE - 1e-12) or (anneal and T>1e-8 and rng.random() < math.exp(-(newE-curE)/T))
          if accepted:
              c0=newc0; curE=newE
              if newE<best_E:
                 best_E=newE; best_h=h.copy()
              if newE < old_cur? no
              if newE < oldcur? not
              noimp = 0 if newE < curE_old? careful curE updated. Use oldE=curE before.
          else:
              pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; h[i]=oldh_i; h[j]=oldh_j; c0=oldc0
              noimp +=1
       else:
          # try multiple fractions
          oldh_i=hi; oldh_j=hj; oldc0=c0; pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy()
          best_t=0.0; best_trial_E=curE - 1e-15? Need allow improvement only; for anneal maybe best of tried.
          # For deterministic, just best improvement.
          # Generate fracs: [0.9,0.5,0.25,0.1] maybe; if tries=3 use first 3.
          fracs = (0.8, 0.4, 0.15) if tries==3 else (0.6,0.3)
          for fr in fracs:
              t=sign*tmax*fr
              if abs(t)<eps: continue
              newc0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,i,j,t)
              newE=max(newc0,pos.max(),neg.max())
              if newE < best_trial_E:
                  best_trial_E=newE; best_t=t; best_trial_c0=newc0
              pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; h[i]=oldh_i; h[j]=oldh_j
          h currently old; if best_t !=0:

              newE=best_trial_E (or recompute to be safe)
              # accept if improves; if anneal maybe also accept if not worse? best_trial might be worse than curE? We set best_trial_E initial curE not allowing worse with no improvement? For deterministic only if <curE. For anneal, could accept if probabilistic. But tries with anneal not used. We'll accept if newE < curE -1e-12; else rollback.

Let's write `update_candidates` top-level:
```
def update_candidates(h,score,n,binary,k):
    if binary:
       zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
       ones=np.nonzero(h>=0.5)[0]
       k1=min(k,len(zeros)); k2=min(k,len(ones))
       zlow=zeros[np.argsort(score[zeros])[:k1]] if len(zeros) else np.array([],int)
       ohigh=ones[np.argsort(score[ones])[::-1][:k2]] if len(ones) else np.array([],int)
       return zlow,ohigh,None
    else:
       rank=np.argsort(score)
       k1=min(k,n)
       return rank[:k1],rank[n-k1:],rank
```
For binary, if h after reset has not exact 0/1? We call to_binary. Good.

Now, verify apply_pair with c0 parameter. Code:
```
def apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0,i,j,t):
    n=len(h)
    hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
    p=i; dlt=t
    if p>0:
       rev=h[:p][::-1]
       pos[1:p+1] -= dlt*rev
       neg[1:p+1] += dlt
       neg[1:p+1] -= dlt*rev
    if p+1<n:
       plus=h[p+1:]
       pos[1:n-p] += dlt
       pos[1:n-p] -= dlt*plus
       neg[1:n-p] -= dlt*plus
    p=j; dlt=-t
    ...
    d=abs(i-j)
    if d>0:
       tt=t*t; pos[d]+=tt; neg[d]+=tt
    newc0 = c0 + t*(1.0-2.0*hi) - t*(1.0-2.0*hj) - 2.0*t*t
    h[i]=hi+t; h[j]=hj-t
    return newc0
```
Check plus slice length: if p+1<n, n-p-1 length. `pos[1:n-p]` length (n-p)-1 = n-p-1. Good. If p=n-1, p+1<n false, skip. If p=0, first skip; second `pos[1:n]` length n-1, `plus=h[1:]` length n-1.

When p>0, `neg[1:p+1] += dlt` adds scalar to slice. If dlt is -t, works. `neg[1:p+1] -= dlt*rev`: if dlt negative, subtract negative = add. Good.
Potential repeated slice assignments with `dlt*rev` create array length p; okay.

`compute_score`: Need include c0 if a lot. We use `tol=1.0` maybe. For continuous n=400, count max ~76, active maybe several within 1. For binary integer, tol=0.75 means only active with exact E? If E integer, pos[d]>=E-0.75 includes only E (since next E-1 < E-0.75). Good. For continuous, includes near active. Maybe set tol=0.75. If too few, fallback.
In compute_score, weights `w1 = pos[d] - thresh`. If pos[d]=E, w1=0.75; if E-0.5, w1=0.25. Good. For c0, if c0 close but less than threshold, skip. If c0 is only max, included.
If no active due thresh > all? E is max, so at least one val = E, w=tol>0. Unless E from pos but pos max exactly; then active. So fallback rarely. But if E is c0 and c0=door, included. Good.
`score -= score.mean()` after weighting. If all score zero? mean zero.

`update_candidates`: For continuous, k = max(10,n//6). If n=400, 66. For n=300,50. Good.
For binary, zeros/ones. If `score` length n. `np.argsort(score[zeros])` returns indices in zero subset; applying to zeros: `zeros[order[:k]]`. Need `order = np.argsort(score[zeros])`; if zeros size >k. `ohigh = ones[np.argsort(score[ones])[::-1][:k]]`. Good.

   low, high, _ = cands
   i=int(rng.choice(low)); j=int(rng.choice(high))
   if i==j: steps+=1; continue
   hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]

      tneg = min(max(0.0,hi), max(0.0,1.0-hj))
      if tpos > eps: sign=1; tmax=tpos

   if tries > 1:

      else: fr=0.05

Let's draft `local_search` code carefully with variables.

Pseudo full:
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,seed,binary=False,anneal=False,tries=1):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if binary and n%2==0: h=to_binary(h,n)
    else: binary=False
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n)
    curE=current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
    best_E=curE; best_h=h.copy()
    k=max(5,n//6)
    score,cands=rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
    T=(0.003 if binary else 0.0009)*(n/2.0) if anneal else 0.0
    cool=0.99975
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    noimp=0; steps=0; eps=1e-12
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if steps % 1000 == 0:
          score,cands=rescore(...)
       if binary:
          zlow,ohigh,_=cands
          if zlow.size==0 or ohigh.size==0: break
          if anneal and rng.random()<0.12 and zlow.size>1 and ohigh.size>1:
             # 2-swap
             i1=int(rng.choice(zlow)); j1=int(rng.choice(ohigh)); i2=int(rng.choice(zlow)); j2=int(rng.choice(ohigh))
             if i1!=i2 and j1!=j2 and i1!=j1 and i1!=j2 and i2!=j1 and i2!=j2:
                h_old=h.copy(); pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy(); old_c0=c0; old_E=curE
                c0t=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,old_c0,i1,j1,1.0)
                nE0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0t,i2,j2,1.0)
                nE=max(nE0,pos.max(),neg.max())
                accepted = nE < old_E -1e-12
                if not accepted and anneal and T>1e-8:
                   dE=nE-old_E; accepted = dE<=0 or rng.random()<math.exp(-min(dE,50)/T)
                if accepted:
                   c0=nE0; curE=nE
                   if nE<best_E: best_E=nE; best_h=h.copy()
                   noimp = 0 if nE<old_E-1e-12 else noimp+1
                else:
                   h[:]=h_old; pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; c0=old_c0; noimp+=1
                T=max(1e-4,T*cool)
                if noimp > 10000:
                   score,cands=rescore(...)
                   noimp=0
                   if anneal:
                      T=max(T, 0.003*(n/2.0))
                      if curE > best_E + 2.0:
                         # reset best + flips
                         h[:]=best_h; c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n); curE=best_E
                         zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]
                         nfl=min(8,len(zeros),len(ones))
                         for _ in range(nfl):
                            a=int(rng.choice(zeros)); b=int(rng.choice(ones)); apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0,a,b,1.0)
                         c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n); curE=current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
                         T=0.5*(n/200.0)?
                      score,cands=rescore(...)
                steps+=1
                continue
          i=int(rng.choice(zlow)); j=int(rng.choice(ohigh)); t=1.0
       else:
          low,high,_=cands
          i=int(rng.choice(low)); j=int(rng.choice(high))
          if i==j:
             steps+=1; continue
          hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
          if score[i] <= score[j]:
             tpos = min(max(0.0,1.0-hi), max(0.0,hj)); tneg = min(max(0.0,hi), max(0.0,1.0-hj))
             if tpos>eps: sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
             elif tneg>eps: sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
             else: steps+=1; continue
          else:
             tneg = min(max(0.0,hi), max(0.0,1.0-hj)); tpos = min(max(0.0,1.0-hi), max(0.0,hj))
             if tneg>eps: sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
             elif tpos>eps: sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
             else: steps+=1; continue
          if tries > 1:
             # multi
             ...
             continue
          r=rng.random()
          if r<0.25: fr=1.0
          elif r<0.55: fr=0.5
          elif r<0.85: fr=0.2
          else: fr=0.05
          if anneal and rng.random()<0.2: fr=0.05+0.95*rng.random()
          t=sign*tmax*fr
          if abs(t)<=eps: steps+=1; continue
       # common single
       old_h_i=h[i]; old_h_j=h[j]; old_c0=c0; old_E=curE
       pos_old=pos.copy(); neg_old=neg.copy()
       nE0=apply_pair(h,pos,neg,old_c0,i,j,t)
       nE=max(nE0,pos.max(),neg.max())
       accepted = nE < old_E - 1e-12
       if not accepted and anneal and T>1e-8:
          dE=nE-old_E
          if dE <=0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-min(dE,50.0)/T): accepted=True
       if accepted:
          c0=nE0; curE=nE
          if nE<best_E: best_E=nE; best_h=h.copy()
          noimp = 0 if nE < old_E - 1e-12 else noimp+1
          if nE < old_E - 1e-12 and steps % 50 == 0:
             score,cands=rescore(...)
       else:
          pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; h[i]=old_h_i; h[j]=old_h_j; c0=old_c0; noimp +=1
       T=max(1e-4,T*cool)
       if noimp > 10000:
          score,cands=rescore(...)
          noimp=0
          if anneal:
             T=max(T, (0.003 if binary else 0.0009)*(n/2.0))
             if curE > best_E + (2.5 if binary else 1.5):
                if binary:
                   h[:]=best_h; c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n); curE=best_E; flips...
                else:
                   h[:] = project_feasible(best_h + 0.02*rng.randn(n), n)
                   c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n); curE=current_energy(...)
                T=(0.005 if binary else 0.0015)*(n/2.0)
                score,cands=rescore(...)
       steps +=1
    return best_h,best_E
```
In 2-swap accepted worse, we set `noimp +=1`; if nE improved but not best? noimp=0. Good.
For binary multi reset flips, `apply_pair` returns new c0 but we ignore? Need update c0 sequentially. In flip loop:

def resample(h,n,m):
  if n==m: return h.copy()
  x0=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
  x1=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m
  return np.interp(x1,x0,h)
```

Let's implement project_feasible more efficiently and exact:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    s=h.sum()
    diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: return h
    # Use bisection for large diff
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # adjust residual exactly
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
    else:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    for k in range(n):
       p=int(idx[k])
       if diff>0:
          amt=min(1-h[p], diff)
          h[p]+=amt; diff-=amt
       else:
          amt=min(h[p], -diff)
          h[p]-=amt; diff+=amt
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return h
```
This might adjust many values if diff large. Good. But bisection with tau only works if target within [0,n]? yes. If input all 0 and target n/2, tau at 0.5. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
        else:
            idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
        for k in range(n):
            p = int(idx[k])
            if diff > 0:
                amt = min(1.0 - h[p], diff)
                h[p] += amt
                diff -= amt
            else:
                amt = min(h[p], -diff)
                h[p] -= amt
                diff += amt
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
    return h

...
```
`min` with numpy scalar okay. If diff NaN? no.

`compute_counts`:
```
def compute_counts(h,n):
    oh = 1.0 - h
    c0 = float(np.dot(h, oh))
    pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for d in range(1, n):
        pos[d] = float(np.dot(h[:n-d], oh[d:]))
        neg[d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], oh[:n-d]))
    return c0,pos,neg
```

`current_energy`.

`apply_pair` as above.

`compute_score`: use default tol=0.75.
```
def compute_score(h,c0,pos,neg,E,n,tol=0.75):
    score=np.zeros(n,float)
    thr=E-tol
    w=max(0.0,c0-thr)
    if w>0: score += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
    for d in range(1,n):
       w=pos[d]-thr
       if w>0:
          score[:n-d] += w*(1.0-h[d:])
          score[d:] -= w*h[:n-d]
       w=neg[d]-thr
       if w>0:
          score[d:] += w*(1.0-h[:n-d])
          score[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
    return score
```
No fallback needed because at least one >0? If E is max and thr<E, max val - thr = tol >0 unless tol negative. tol positive. Good. If E is nan? no.

`update_candidates`.
```
def update_candidates(h,score,n,binary,k):
    if binary:
       zeros=np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
       ones=np.nonzero(h >= 0.5)[0]
       k1=min(k,len(zeros)); k2=min(k,len(ones))
       zlow=zeros[np.argsort(score[zeros])[:k1]] if k1 else np.array([],dtype=int)
       ohigh=ones[np.argsort(score[ones])[::-1][:k2]] if k2 else np.array([],dtype=int)
       return zlow,ohigh,None
    rank=np.argsort(score)
    k1=min(k,n)
    return rank[:k1], rank[n-k1:], None
```
If binary and h continuous not exact? We call to_binary. For h values maybe 0.5? If 0.5, which list? zeros <0.5, ones >=0.5. If h not binary due start? local_search binary will to_binary. For reset, binary. Good. If continuous search, binary false.

`rescore` calls compute_score and update.

`to_binary`, `resample`, `final_eval`.

`local_search` full. Need ensure `best_h` is a numpy array. In continuous reset, `h[:] = project_feasible(...)`. If project returns shape same. Good.

Let's write local_search with attention to variable scopes.

At start:
```
def local_search(h, n, deadline, seed, binary=False, anneal=False, tries=1):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if binary and n % 2 == 0:
        h = to_binary(h, n)
    else:
        binary = False
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n)
    curE = current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
    best_E = curE
    best_h = h.copy()
    k = max(5, n // 6)
    score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
    T = (0.003 if binary else 0.0009) * (n / 2.0) if anneal else 0.0
    cool = 0.99975
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    noimp = 0
    steps = 0
    eps = 1e-12
    t_start = (0.003 if binary else 0.0009) * (n / 2.0)
    reset_gap = 12000
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if steps % 1000 == 0:
            score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
        if binary:
            zlow,ohigh,_ = cands
            if zlow.size == 0 or ohigh.size == 0:
                break
            if rng.random() < 0.12 and zlow.size > 1 and ohigh.size > 1:
                i1 = int(rng.choice(zlow)); j1=int(rng.choice(ohigh)); i2=int(rng.choice(zlow)); j2=int(rng.choice(ohigh))
                if i1 != i2 and j1 != j2 and i1 != j1 and i1 != j2 and i2 != j1 and i2 != j2:
                    h_old = h.copy(); pos_old = pos.copy(); neg_old = neg.copy()
                    old_c0 = c0; old_E = curE
                    c0t = apply_pair(h,pos,neg,old_c0,i1,j1,1.0)
                    nE0 = apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0t,i2,j2,1.0)
                    nE = max(nE0, float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
                    accepted = (nE < old_E - 1e-12)
                    if (not accepted) and anneal and T > 1e-8:
                        dE = nE - old_E
                        if dE <= 0.0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-min(dE, 50.0) / T):
                            accepted = True
                    if accepted:
                        c0 = nE0; curE = nE
                        if nE < best_E:
                            best_E = nE; best_h = h.copy()
                        noimp = 0 if (nE < old_E - 1e-12) else noimp + 1
                    else:
                        h[:]=h_old; pos[:]=pos_old; neg[:]=neg_old; c0=old_c0; noimp += 1
                    T = max(1e-5, T * cool)
                    if noimp > reset_gap:
                        score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
                        noimp = 0
                        if anneal:
                            T = max(T, t_start)
                            if curE > best_E + 2.5:
                                h[:] = best_h
                                c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = best_E
                                zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]; ones = np.nonzero(h >= 0.5)[0]
                                nfl = min(8, len(zeros), len(ones))
                                c0tmp = c0
                                for _ in range(nfl):
                                    a = int(rng.choice(zeros)); b = int(rng.choice(ones))
                                    c0tmp = apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0tmp,a,b,1.0)
                                c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
                                T = 2.0 * t_start
                                score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
                    steps += 1
                    continue
            i = int(rng.choice(zlow)); j = int(rng.choice(ohigh)); t = 1.0
        else:
            low,high,_ = cands
            if low.size == 0 or high.size == 0:
                break
            i = int(rng.choice(low)); j = int(rng.choice(high))
            if i == j:
                noimp += 1; steps += 1; continue
            hi = h[i]; hj = h[j]
            if score[i] <= score[j]:
                tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                if tpos > eps:
                    sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
                elif tneg > eps:
                    sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
                else:
                    noimp += 1; steps += 1; continue
            else:
                tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                if tneg > eps:
                    sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
                elif tpos > eps:
                    sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
                else:
                    noimp += 1; steps += 1; continue
            # occasional opposite in anneal
            if anneal and rng.random() < 0.1:
                if sign > 0:
                    tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                    if tneg > eps: sign=-1.0; tmax=tneg
                else:
                    tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                    if tpos > eps: sign=1.0; tmax=tpos
            if tries > 1:
                old_hi = h[i]; old_hj = h[j]; old_c0 = c0
                pos_old = pos.copy(); neg_old = neg.copy(); old_E = curE
                best_t = 0.0; best_nE = old_E; best_nc0 = old_c0
                fracs = (0.75, 0.35, 0.12) if tries >= 3 else (0.6, 0.25)
                for fr in fracs:
                    tt = sign * tmax * fr
                    if abs(tt) <= eps:
                        continue
                    nc0 = apply_pair(h, pos, neg, old_c0, i, j, tt)
                    nE = max(nc0, float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
                    if nE < best_nE - 1e-12:
                        best_nE = nE; best_nc0 = nc0; best_t = tt
                    pos[:] = pos_old; neg[:] = neg_old; h[i] = old_hi; h[j] = old_hj
                if best_t != 0.0:
                    nE = best_nE
                    nc0 = apply_pair(h, pos, neg, old_c0, i, j, best_t)
                    if nE < old_E - 1e-12:
                        c0 = nc0; curE = nE; noimp = 0
                        if nE < best_E:
                            best_E = nE; best_h = h.copy()
                        if steps % 50 == 0:
                            score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
                    else:
                        pos[:] = pos_old; neg[:] = neg_old; h[i] = old_hi; h[j] = old_hj; c0 = old_c0; noimp += 1
                else:
                    noimp += 1
                T = max(1e-5, T * cool)
                if noimp > reset_gap:
                    score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
                    noimp = 0
                    if anneal:
                        T = max(T, t_start)
                        if curE > best_E + 1.5:
                            h[:] = project_feasible(best_h + 0.02 * rng.randn(n), n)
                            c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
                            T = 2.0 * t_start
                            score,cands = rescore(h,c0,pos,neg,curE,n,binary,k)
                steps += 1
                continue
            r = rng.random()
            if r < 0.25:
                fr = 1.0
            elif r < 0.55:
                fr = 0.5
            elif r < 0.85:
                fr = 0.2
            else:
                fr = 0.05
            if anneal and rng.random() < 0.25:
                fr = 0.05 + 0.95 * rng.random()
            t = sign * tmax * fr
            if abs(t) <= eps:
                noimp += 1; steps += 1; continue
        old_hi = h[i]; old_hj = h[j]; old_c0 = c0; old_E = curE
        pos_old = pos.copy(); neg_old = neg.copy()
        nc0 = apply_pair(h, pos, neg, old_c0, i, j, t)
        nE = max(nc0, float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
        accepted = (nE < old_E - 1e-12)
        if (not accepted) and anneal and T > 1e-8:
            dE = nE - old_E
            if dE <= 0.0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-min(dE, 50.0) / T):
                accepted = True
        if accepted:
            c0 = nc0; curE = nE
            if nE < best_E:
                best_E = nE; best_h = h.copy()
            noimp = 0 if (nE < old_E - 1e-12) else noimp + 1
            if nE < old_E - 1e-12 and steps % 50 == 0:
                score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
        else:
            pos[:] = pos_old; neg[:] = neg_old; h[i] = old_hi; h[j] = old_hj; c0 = old_c0
            noimp += 1
        T = max(1e-5, T * cool)
        if noimp > reset_gap:
            score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
            noimp = 0
            if anneal:
                T = max(T, t_start)
                if curE > best_E + (2.5 if binary else 1.5):
                    if binary:
                        h[:] = best_h
                        c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = best_E
                        zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
                        ones = np.nonzero(h >= 0.5)[0]
                        nfl = min(8, len(zeros), len(ones))
                        c0tmp = c0
                        for _ in range(nfl):
                            a = int(rng.choice(zeros)); b = int(rng.choice(ones))
                            c0tmp = apply_pair(h,pos,neg,c0tmp,a,b,1.0)
                        c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
                        T = 2.0 * t_start
                    else:
                        h[:] = project_feasible(best_h + 0.02 * rng.randn(n), n)
                        c0,pos,neg = compute_counts(h,n); curE = current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
                        T = 2.0 * t_start
                    score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
        steps += 1
    return best_h, best_E
```

Now, `refine_candidate`:
```
def refine_candidate(h_start,n,deadline,seed,binary=False,anneal=False,tries=1):
    if time.time() >= deadline - 0.5:
       h = project_feasible(h_start,n)
       c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n)
       return h,current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
    h,best_E=local_search(h_start,n,deadline,seed,binary,anneal,tries)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c0,pos,neg=compute_counts(h,n)
    return h,current_energy(c0,pos,neg)
```
If `local_search` binary false due odd n and then project; okay.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    total=max(10.0,budget_s-5.0)
    end=t0+total
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n0=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       n0=400; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
    if n0 < 2:
       n0=2; h0=np.array([1.0,0.0])
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0.copy()
    best_c=final_eval(best_h,n0)
    best_n=n0
    span=total
    p1=t0+0.18*span
    h,e=refine_candidate(best_h,best_n,min(p1,end),seed+1,FALSE,FALSE,2)
    if current_energy? e count. c=e*2.0/n
    if e*2.0/best_n < best_c:
       best_c=e*2.0/best_n; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=best_n
    p2=t0+0.55*span
    h,e=refine_candidate(best_h,best_n,min(p2,end),seed+2,FALSE,TRUE,1)
    if e*2.0/best_n < best_c: update
    if best_n % 2 == 0:
       p3=t0+0.78*span
       hb=to_binary(best_h,best_n)
       h,e=refine_candidate(hb,best_n,min(p3,end),seed+3,TRUE,TRUE,1)
       if e*2.0/best_n < best_c: update
    # others
    for n2 in (300,500,250):
       if time.time() > end - 80: break
       # choose h_start from best_h resampled
       if n2 != best_n:
           hstart=resample(best_h,best_n,n2)
       else:
           hstart=best_h.copy()
       dur=min(70.0, max(15.0, (end-time.time())/2.0 - 30.0))
       dl=time.time()+dur
       h,e=refine_candidate(hstart,n2,dl,seed+n2,FALSE,TRUE,2)
       ce=e*2.0/n2
       if ce < best_c: update best_h=h.copy(); best_c=ce; best_n=n2
       if n2 %2==0 and time.time() < end - 35:
           hb=to_binary(h,n2)
           dl2=time.time()+min(25.0, end-time.time()-15.0)
           h2,e2=refine_candidate(hb,n2,dl2,seed+n2+1,TRUE,TRUE,1)
           ce2=e2*2.0/n2
           if ce2 < best_c: update
    # final polish
    if time.time() < end - 5:
       h,e=refine_candidate(best_h,best_n,end-2,seed+100,FALSE,FALSE,2)
       ce=e*2.0/best_n
       if ce < best_c: update
       if best_n %2==0 and time.time() < end - 3:
          hb=to_binary(best_h,best_n)
          h2,e2=refine_candidate(hb,best_n,end-1,seed+101,TRUE,FALSE,1)
          ce2=e2*2.0/best_n
          if ce2 < best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=final_eval(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n

Let's draft simplified local_search:
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,seed,binary=False,anneal=False):
    h=... to_binary/project
    c0,pos,neg=...
    curE=...
    best_E=curE; best_h=h.copy()
    k=max(10,n//5)
    score,cands=rescore(...)
    T=(0.003 if binary else 0.0009)*n/2 if anneal else 0.0
    t_start=T; cool=0.9997
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    noimp=0; steps=0; eps=1e-12
    while time<deadline:
       if steps % 700 ==0: score,cands=rescore(...)
       if binary:
          zlow,ohigh,_=cands
          if zlow.size==0 or ohigh.size==0: break
          i=choice(zlow); j=choice(ohigh); t=1.0
       else:
          low,high,_=cands
          i=choice(low); j=choice(high)
          if i==j: noimp+=1; steps+=1; continue
          hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
          # primary direction by score
          if score[i] <= score[j]:
             tpos=min(max(0,1-hi),max(0,hj)); tneg=min(max(0,hi),max(0,1-hj))
             if tpos>eps: sign=1; tmax=tpos
             elif tneg>eps: sign=-1; tmax=tneg
             else: noimp+=1; steps+=1; continue
          else:
             tneg=...; tpos=...
             if tneg>eps: sign=-1; tmax=tneg
             elif tpos>eps: sign=1; tmax=tpos
             else: noimp...
          if anneal and rng.random()<0.12: # occasionally opposite
             ...
          r=rng.random()
          if not anneal:
             fr = 1.0 if r<0.45 else 0.5 if r<0.8 else 0.2
          else:
             if r<0.15: fr=1.0
             elif r<0.4: fr=0.5
             elif r<0.7: fr=0.2
             else: fr=0.05 + 0.95*rng.random()
          t=sign*tmax*fr
          if abs(t)<=eps: noimp+=1; steps+=1; continue
       old...
       pos_old...
       nc0=apply_pair(...)
       nE=max(...)
       accepted=...
       if accepted: update; noimp=0 if improve else +1; if improve and steps%30==0 rescore
       else rollback; noimp+=1
       T=max(1e-5,T*cool)
       if noimp > 10000:
          score,cands=rescore(...)
          noimp=0
          if anneal:
             T=max(T,t_start)
             if curE > best_E + (2.5 if binary else 1.5):
                if binary: h[:]=best_h; c0,pos,neg=compute; curE=best_E; flips 8; recompute; T=2*t_start
                else: h[:]=project(best_h+0.02randn); recompute; T=2*t_start
             score,cands=rescore(...)
       steps+=1
    return best_h,best_E
```
This is ~120 lines. Good.
Runs more iterations because no multi copies. Each candidate copy once, one apply. 1M candidates possible.
Will it find fine improvements? The score-guided random pair moves with anneal should. For deterministic phase, it will try moves from low score to high score with random fraction. If a small move improves, it may choose large fraction and worsen; but deterministic rejects and noimp. It may need to hit small fraction. Probability small fraction 20%; okay. We can in deterministic use smaller fractions more often: `fr = 0.2 if r<0.4 else 0.5 if r<0.7 else 1.0`? Since near optimum small moves. Use:
- deterministic: r<0.4 ->0.15; r<0.7 ->0.4; else 1.0.
- anneal: r<0.15 ->1.0; r<0.4 ->0.4; r<0.7 ->0.15; else random.
This increases chance. Good.

Continuous phase allocation can be increased since per iter fast.

Now, can deterministic score-guided no anneal get stuck if no improving move found by sampling? It will noimp reset recompute score but no reheat/reset (anneal false). It may loop local. Then anneal phase from best. Good.

Run phases with simplified:
- p1 continuous deterministic 0.20 span
- p2 continuous anneal 0.55 span
- p3 binary anneal 0.78 span
- others/final
This should be enough.

Potential issue: For deterministic no worse, accepting a move that improves current E but maybe pushes away from best? It can't be worse than current, but objective nonconvex can get into lower but not global? It tracks best; if it wanders to lower but then stuck, best remains. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        idx = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1] if diff > 0 else np.argsort(h)[::-1]
        for k in range(n):
            p = int(idx[k])
            if diff > 0:
                amt = min(1.0 - h[p], diff)
                h[p] += amt
                diff -= amt
            else:
                amt = min(h[p], -diff)
                h[p] -= amt
                diff += amt
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
    return h


def compute_counts(h, n):
    oh = 1.0 - h
    c0 = float(np.dot(h, oh))
    pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for d in range(1, n):
        pos[d] = float(np.dot(h[:n - d], oh[d:]))
        neg[d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], oh[:n - d]))
    return c0, pos, neg


def current_energy(c0, pos, neg):
    return max(float(c0), float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))


def apply_pair(h, pos, neg, c0, i, j, t):
    n = len(h)
    hi = h[i]
    hj = h[j]

    p = i
    dlt = t
    if p > 0:
        rev = h[:p][::-1]
        pos[1:p + 1] -= dlt * rev
        neg[1:p + 1] += dlt
        neg[1:p + 1] -= dlt * rev
    if p + 1 < n:
        plus = h[p + 1:]
        pos[1:n - p] += dlt
        pos[1:n - p] -= dlt * plus
        neg[1:n - p] -= dlt * plus

    p = j
    dlt = -t
    if p > 0:
        rev = h[:p][::-1]
        pos[1:p + 1] -= dlt * rev
        neg[1:p + 1] += dlt
        neg[1:p + 1] -= dlt * rev
    if p + 1 < n:
        plus = h[p + 1:]
        pos[1:n - p] += dlt
        pos[1:n - p] -= dlt * plus
        neg[1:n - p] -= dlt * plus

    d = abs(i - j)
    if d > 0:
        tt = t * t
        pos[d] += tt
        neg[d] += tt

    new_c0 = c0 + t * (1.0 - 2.0 * hi) - t * (1.0 - 2.0 * hj) - 2.0 * t * t
    h[i] = hi + t
    h[j] = hj - t
    return new_c0


def compute_score(h, c0, pos, neg, E, n, tol=0.75):
    score = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    thr = E - tol
    w = max(0.0, c0 - thr)
    if w > 0.0:
        score += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    for d in range(1, n):
        w = pos[d] - thr
        if w > 0.0:
            score[:n - d] += w * (1.0 - h[d:])
            score[d:] -= w * h[:n - d]
        w = neg[d] - thr
        if w > 0.0:
            score[d:] += w * (1.0 - h[:n - d])
            score[:n - d] -= w * h[d:]
    return score


def update_candidates(h, score, n, binary, k):
    if binary:
        zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.nonzero(h >= 0.5)[0]
        k1 = min(k, len(zeros))
        k2 = min(k, len(ones))
        if k1:
            zlow = zeros[np.argsort(score[zeros])[:k1]]
        else:
            zlow = np.array([], dtype=int)
        if k2:
            ohigh = ones[np.argsort(score[ones])[::-1][:k2]]
        else:
            ohigh = np.array([], dtype=int)
        return zlow, ohigh, None
    rank = np.argsort(score)
    k1 = min(k, n)
    return rank[:k1], rank[n - k1:], None


def rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, E, n, binary, k):
    sc = compute_score(h, c0, pos, neg, E, n)
    return sc, update_candidates(h, sc, n, binary, k)


def to_binary(h, n):
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return b


def resample(h, n, m):
    if n == m:
        return h.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x1 = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h)


def final_eval(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def local_search(h, n, deadline, seed, binary=False, anneal=False):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if binary and n % 2 == 0:
        h = to_binary(h, n)
    else:
        binary = False
    h = project_feasible(h, n)

    c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
    curE = current_energy(c0, pos, neg)
    best_E = curE
    best_h = h.copy()

    k = max(10, n // 5)
    score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)

    t_start = (0.003 if binary else 0.0009) * (n / 2.0)
    T = t_start if anneal else 0.0
    cool = 0.9997
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    noimp = 0
    steps = 0
    eps = 1e-12

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if steps % 700 == 0:
            score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)

        if binary:
            zlow, ohigh, _ = cands
            if zlow.size == 0 or ohigh.size == 0:
                break
            i = int(rng.choice(zlow))
            j = int(rng.choice(ohigh))
            t = 1.0
        else:
            low, high, _ = cands
            if low.size == 0 or high.size == 0:
                break
            i = int(rng.choice(low))
            j = int(rng.choice(high))
            if i == j:
                noimp += 1
                steps += 1
                continue

            hi = h[i]
            hj = h[j]
            if score[i] <= score[j]:
                tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                if tpos > eps:
                    sign = 1.0
                    tmax = tpos
                elif tneg > eps:
                    sign = -1.0
                    tmax = tneg
                else:
                    noimp += 1
                    steps += 1
                    continue
            else:
                tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                if tneg > eps:
                    sign = -1.0
                    tmax = tneg
                elif tpos > eps:
                    sign = 1.0
                    tmax = tpos
                else:
                    noimp += 1
                    steps += 1
                    continue

            if anneal and rng.random() < 0.12:
                if sign > 0.0:
                    tneg = min(max(0.0, hi), max(0.0, 1.0 - hj))
                    if tneg > eps:
                        sign = -1.0
                        tmax = tneg
                else:
                    tpos = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - hi), max(0.0, hj))
                    if tpos > eps:
                        sign = 1.0
                        tmax = tpos

            r = rng.random()
            if not anneal:
                if r < 0.45:
                    fr = 0.15
                elif r < 0.75:
                    fr = 0.4
                else:
                    fr = 1.0
            else:
                if r < 0.15:
                    fr = 1.0
                elif r < 0.40:
                    fr = 0.4
                elif r < 0.70:
                    fr = 0.15
                else:
                    fr = 0.05 + 0.95 * rng.random()

            t = sign * tmax * fr
            if abs(t) <= eps:
                noimp += 1
                steps += 1
                continue

        old_hi = h[i]
        old_hj = h[j]
        old_c0 = c0
        old_E = curE
        pos_old = pos.copy()
        neg_old = neg.copy()

        new_c0 = apply_pair(h, pos, neg, old_c0, i, j, t)
        new_E = max(new_c0, float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))

        accepted = new_E < old_E - 1e-12
        if (not accepted) and anneal and T > 1e-8:
            de = new_E - old_E
            if de <= 0.0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-min(de, 50.0) / T):
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            c0 = new_c0
            curE = new_E
            if new_E < best_E:
                best_E = new_E
                best_h = h.copy()
            noimp = 0 if (new_E < old_E - 1e-12) else noimp + 1
            if new_E < old_E - 1e-12 and steps % 30 == 0:
                score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
        else:
            pos[:] = pos_old
            neg[:] = neg_old
            h[i] = old_hi
            h[j] = old_hj
            c0 = old_c0
            noimp += 1

        if anneal:
            T = max(1e-5, T * cool)

        if noimp > 10000:
            score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)
            noimp = 0
            if anneal:
                T = max(T, t_start)
                if curE > best_E + (2.5 if binary else 1.5):
                    if binary:
                        h[:] = best_h
                        c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
                        curE = best_E
                        zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
                        ones = np.nonzero(h >= 0.5)[0]
                        nfl = min(8, len(zeros), len(ones))
                        c0tmp = c0
                        for _ in range(nfl):
                            a = int(rng.choice(zeros))
                            b = int(rng.choice(ones))
                            c0tmp = apply_pair(h, pos, neg, c0tmp, a, b, 1.0)
                        c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
                        curE = current_energy(c0, pos, neg)
                        T = 2.0 * t_start
                    else:
                        h[:] = project_feasible(best_h + 0.02 * rng.randn(n), n)
                        c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
                        curE = current_energy(c0, pos, neg)
                        T = 2.0 * t_start
                    score, cands = rescore(h, c0, pos, neg, curE, n, binary, k)

        steps += 1

    return best_h, best_E


def refine_candidate(h_start, n, deadline, seed, binary=False, anneal=False):
    if time.time() >= deadline - 0.5:
        h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
        c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
        return h, current_energy(c0, pos, neg)
    h, best_E = local_search(h_start, n, deadline, seed, binary, anneal)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c0, pos, neg = compute_counts(h, n)
    return h, current_energy(c0, pos, neg)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    end = t0 + total

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    if n0 < 2:
        n0 = 2
        h0 = np.array([1.0, 0.0], dtype=float)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = final_eval(best_h, best_n)

    span = end - t0

    p1 = t0 + 0.20 * span
    h, e = refine_candidate(best_h, best_n, min(p1, end), seed + 1, False, False)
    ce = e * (2.0 / best_n)
    if ce < best_c:
        best_c = ce
        best_h = h.copy()

    p2 = t0 + 0.55 * span
    h, e = refine_candidate(best_h, best_n, min(p2, end), seed + 2, False, True)
    ce = e * (2.0 / best_n)
    if ce < best_c:
        best_c = ce
        best_h = h.copy()

    if best_n % 2 == 0:
        p3 = t0 + 0.78 * span
        hb = to_binary(best_h, best_n)
        h, e = refine_candidate(hb, best_n, min(p3, end), seed + 3, True, True)
        ce = e * (2.0 / best_n)
        if ce < best_c:
            best_c = ce
            best_h = h.copy()

    for n2 in (300, 500, 250):
        if time.time() > end - 80:
            break
        if n2 != best_n:
            hstart = resample(best_h, best_n, n2)
        else:
            hstart = best_h.copy()
        rem = end - time.time()
        dur = min(70.0, max(15.0, 0.5 * rem - 30.0))
        dl = time.time() + dur
        h, e = refine_candidate(hstart, n2, dl, seed + n2, False, True)
        ce = e * (2.0 / n2)
        if ce < best_c:
            best_c = ce
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n2
        if n2 % 2 == 0 and time.time() < end - 35:
            hb2 = to_binary(h, n2)
            dl2 = time.time() + min(25.0, end - time.time() - 15.0)
            h2, e2 = refine_candidate(hb2, n2, dl2, seed + n2 + 1, True, True)
            ce2 = e2 * (2.0 / n2)
            if ce2 < best_c:
                best_c = ce2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_n = n2

    if time.time() < end - 5:
        h, e = refine_candidate(best_h, best_n, end - 2, seed + 100, False, False)
        ce = e * (2.0 / best_n)
        if ce < best_c:
            best_c = ce
            best_h = h.copy()

        if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < end - 3:
            hb = to_binary(best_h, best_n)
            h2, e2 = refine_candidate(hb, best_n, end - 1, seed + 101, True, False)
            ce2 = e2 * (2.0 / best_n)
            if ce2 < best_c:
                best_c = ce2
                best_h = h2.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = final_eval(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
